SELECT [m].[ID], [m].[CountryID], [m].[ManufacturerName], [c].[ID], [c].[EnComponentKind], [c].[RuComponentKind], [c0].[ID], [c0].[EnComponentType], [c0].[RuComponentType], [r].[ComponentName]
FROM [Resistors] AS [r]
LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m] ON [r].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c] ON [r].[Kind_ID] = [c].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c0] ON [r].[Type_ID] = [c0].[ID]
